# Full release restoration, 2026-10-15

The expanded release restores the exact historically validated instructions while retaining current image locations. The patcher now accepts `--restore-historical-instructions CURRENT_TASKS` alongside `--validated-tasks HISTORICAL_TASKS`, `--validated-archive HISTORICAL_ARCHIVE`, `--run-record HISTORICAL_RUN`, `--publication-revision IMMUTABLE_REVISION` and `--output NEW_DIRECTORY`.

`data/utils/historical_release.py` compares every runtime file and mode against historical evidence, validates content hashes, restores only instruction payload differences and updates cached-image task digests. Historical stages 1, 3, 4 and 5 are preserved; no fresh runtime pass is claimed. Retained/archive IDs and all archived payloads remain unchanged. The published patcher includes this helper as `assets/historical_release.py`. Set `PYTHONPATH=/path/to/OT_next_data` when running a downloaded patcher so its existing shared validation/reporting imports resolve.

Audit artifacts: `/hnvme/workspace/y500bb12-optiagent/full-release-audit-20261015/scaleswe/`. `restoration-audit.json` records task-level hash changes; `historical-hf-verification.json` pins downloaded evidence.

The sections below describe the earlier environment-only variant and its repair provenance. Their instruction-removal instructions are historical; use the restoration mode above for the expanded release.

---

# Scale SWE patch details

## Provenance and accounting

The environment-reliable candidate contains 16,786 tasks: 456 have runtime repairs
and 16,330 reproduce the original converted task archives byte for byte. The 416
excluded tasks retain their original payloads and reasons in `archive.parquet`.
Their historical archive stage remains 0 (patch-time exclusion); the README assigns
each exclusion once by its recorded cause. Repairs affecting both reference and
no-op execution are counted under reference, avoiding overlapping totals.
Every assigned repair stage has a saved failed report for that task.

The historical publication changed 1,197 retained tasks before automatic suffix
addition. Of these, 741 had only instruction-path edits, 447 had only runtime
repairs, and 9 had both. Removing instruction-only changes leaves 456 patched tasks.
All 750 path edits and all 16,786 automatic instruction suffixes are excluded.
No additional task-quality static exclusions were applied.

All 17,202 original Harbor payload hashes were reproduced from the pinned Prime
source using the original converter support files. Removing just the automatic
suffix reproduces each historical patcher output hash. Restoring the original
instruction then changes no other file or file mode in any retained payload.
The original image references, setup, reference solutions and repaired verifiers
are preserved. The candidate's stages 3/4/5 are transferred historical evidence;
there was no fresh runtime validation or teacher run of this instruction variant.
The earlier full reproduction attempt also did not complete, as documented in
the historical audit.

- [Historical run and patch provenance](https://huggingface.co/datasets/FWeindel/validated-tasks/blob/e77e80246668e100171168fa369012fd68f3d53a/runs/2026-10-14-scaleswe-historical-audit.json)
- [Original stage reports](https://huggingface.co/datasets/FWeindel/validated-tasks/blob/e77e80246668e100171168fa369012fd68f3d53a/runs/2026-10-14-scaleswe-historical-audit/source-reports.json.gz)
- [Per-task selected evidence](https://huggingface.co/datasets/FWeindel/validated-tasks/blob/e77e80246668e100171168fa369012fd68f3d53a/runs/2026-10-14-scaleswe-historical-audit/task-evidence-index.json.gz)

## Images and local files

The Docker Hub repository is [aweaiteam/scaleswe](https://hub.docker.com/r/aweaiteam/scaleswe/tags).
The 17,202 source tags are unique; the retained tasks use 16,786 unique tags.
No image definitions were consolidated or modified. These are the upstream
prebuilt Docker images. Cluster-local Apptainer caches are not uploaded in this
candidate. The tags are the upstream references, not newly resolved immutable
digests. The README's example tag was checked as available during preparation;
this preparation did not repeat an availability check of every tag.

`patch.py` contains both the historical patching path and environment-candidate
preparation. `archived_tasks.json` is a required input for the historical patcher,
containing reviewed task exclusions and their reasons; it is not disposable run output.
Generated Parquets, inputs and audit files are kept outside the repository at:

`/hnvme/workspace/y500bb12-optiagent/scaleswe-environment-reliable-20261015/`

## Reproduce the candidate

Download the two pinned upstream Parquet shards to `input/upstream/data/`, and
`scaleswe/tasks.parquet`, `scaleswe/archive.parquet` and the historical run JSON
from the publication revision linked above to matching paths under `input/`.
Run from the repository root, using a new output directory:

```bash
python -m data.scaleswe.patch \
  --upstream-shards input/upstream/data \
  --validated-tasks input/scaleswe/tasks.parquet \
  --validated-archive input/scaleswe/archive.parquet \
  --run-record input/runs/2026-10-14-scaleswe-historical-audit.json \
  --publication-revision e77e80246668e100171168fa369012fd68f3d53a \
  --output prepared
```

Outputs are `tasks.parquet`, `archive.parquet`, `audit.json`, `summary.json` and
`images.json`. Audit files describe historical evidence transfer explicitly.
No publishing is performed by this command.

## Preparation checks

All 70 focused Scale SWE tests passed. The full repository suite reported
1,839 passed, 1 skipped and the same six failures already present during SWE-Lego
preparation, in image-preparation mocks, runtime-configuration mocks and validation
pipeline expectations. Log: `/tmp/scaleswe-all-tests.log`.
Independent output checks confirmed unique task IDs, counts, repaired flags,
removal of task-quality labels and preservation of exclusion reasons.
